/* ================= SALE ================= */
let SALE_RAW_HDRS = null;
const SALE_RAW_FIELDS = {
  sellerId: ['seller id', 'seller id fk', 'sellerid', 'seller code', 'vendor id', 'vendor code', 'partner id', 'store id'],
  program: ['po type'], warehouseId: ['warehouse id'], orderDate: ['created on'], styleCode: ['style id'],
  sku: ['seller sku code'], skuId: ['sku id'], myntraSku: ['myntra sku code'], size: ['size'],
  vendorArticle: ['vendor article number'], brand: ['brand'], desc: ['style name'], articleType: ['article type', 'product type'],
  city: ['city'], mrp: ['total mrp'], discount: ['discount'], rate: ['final amount'], sp: ['seller price'],
  mpStatus: ['order status'], cancelReason: ['cancellation reason'],
  state: ['state', 'ship state', 'shipping state', 'buyer state', 'destination state', 'state name', 'deliver state']
};
function saleRawFrom(it, names) {
  if (!(it.raw && SALE_RAW_HDRS)) return '';
  const keys = SALE_RAW_HDRS.map(function (h) { return String(h || '').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, ''); });
  for (let n = 0; n < names.length; n++) {
    const nk = String(names[n] || '').replace(/\s+/g, '');
    const i = keys.indexOf(nk);
    if (i > -1 && it.raw[i] != null && it.raw[i] !== '') return it.raw[i];
  }
  return '';
}
function saleRawVal(it, f) {
  if (it[f] != null && it[f] !== '') return it[f];
  return saleRawFrom(it, SALE_RAW_FIELDS[f] || []) || it[f] || '';
}
function saleItemRow(it) {
  it = it || {};
  const tx = function (f, ph) { const v = f === 'desc' ? (saleRawFrom(it, ['style name']) || it.desc || '') : saleRawVal(it, f); return '<td><input class="inp" data-f="' + f + '" value="' + esc(v || '') + '" placeholder="' + (ph || '') + '" spellcheck="false"></td>'; };
  const nm = function (f, ph) { const v = saleRawVal(it, f); return '<td><input class="inp num" type="number" step="any" data-f="' + f + '" value="' + (v === '' || v == null ? '' : v) + '" oninput="calcSale()" placeholder="' + (ph || '') + '"></td>'; };
  return '<tr>' +
    srTd() +
    tx('sellerId', 'e.g. 68905') +
    tx('program', 'PPMP / FBM / SJIT') +
    tx('warehouseId') +
    tx('orderDate') +
    tx('styleCode') +
    tx('sku') +
    tx('skuId') +
    tx('myntraSku') +
    tx('size') +
    tx('vendorArticle') +
    tx('brand') +
    tx('desc', 'Style name') +
    tx('articleType') +
    tx('city') +
    nm('mrp') +
    nm('discount') +
    nm('rate') +
    nm('sp') +
    tx('mpStatus') +
    tx('cancelReason') +
    '<td><input class="inp num" type="number" min="0" data-f="qty" value="' + (it.qty ?? 1) + '" oninput="calcSale()"></td>' +
    '<td class="num" data-amt style="font-weight:700">0</td>' +
    '<td><input type="hidden" data-f="orderNo" value="' + esc(it.orderNo || '') + '">' +
    '<input type="hidden" data-f="orderLine" value="' + esc(it.orderLine || '') + '">' +
    '<button type="button" class="btn-icon danger" onclick="removeRow(this)">' + ic('trash', 15) + '</button></td>' +
  '</tr>';
}
function formSale(rec) {
  const r = rec || {};
  return '<div class="sec">Sale Entry</div><div class="grid2">' +
    field('Sale No (auto)', '<input class="inp" id="f-no" value="' + esc(r.no || '') + '" disabled>') +
    field('Date *', '<input class="inp" type="text" data-date autocomplete="off" readonly id="f-date" value="' + esc(r.date || today()) + '">') +
    field('Notes', '<input class="inp" id="f-notes" value="' + esc(r.notes || '') + '" placeholder="Optional">') +
  '</div>' +
  '<div class="sec">Sale Items — Excel-style entry</div>' +
  '<div class="table-wrap"><table class="mini-table xl-table" style="min-width:2390px"><thead><tr>' + srTh() +
    '<th title="the seller id the Seller \u2194 po_type master keys off">seller id</th><th>po_type</th><th>warehouse id</th><th>created on</th><th>style id</th><th>seller sku code</th><th>sku id</th><th>myntra sku code</th><th>size</th><th>vendor article number</th><th>brand</th><th>style name</th><th>product type</th><th>city</th><th class="num">total mrp</th><th class="num">discount</th><th class="num">final amount</th><th class="num">seller price</th><th>order status</th><th>cancellation reason</th><th class="num">Qty</th><th class="num">Amount</th><th></th>' +
  '</tr></thead>' +
  (SALE_RAW_HDRS = (r.source && r.source.rawHeaders) || null, '') +
  '<tbody id="sale-items">' + (r.items && r.items.length ? r.items.map(saleItemRow).join('') : saleItemRow()) + '</tbody>' +
  '<tfoot><tr class="tot-row"><td></td><td>Total</td><td colspan="19"></td><td class="num" id="sale-tqty">0</td><td class="num" id="sale-tamt">₹0</td><td></td></tr></tfoot></table></div>' +
  '<div class="bom-foot"><span class="bom-hint">Paste rows from Excel · drag the green corner to fill down</span>' +
  '<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="addRow(\'sale-items\',saleItemRow);calcSale()">' + ic('plus',15) + 'Add item</button></div>' +
  '<div class="hint">Status: ' + cbox('sl-status', { opts: STATUSES.sale, value: r.status || 'Open', f:'status', free:false, compact:true }) + '</div>';
}
function saleRowEffRate(tr) {
  const g = function (f) { const el = qs('[data-f="' + f + '"]', tr); return el ? pf(el.value) : 0; };
  let eff = g('rate');
  if (eff <= 0 && (g('mrp') - g('discount')) > 0) eff = g('mrp') - g('discount');
  if (eff <= 0 && g('sp') > 0) eff = g('sp');
  return eff;
}
function calcSale() {
  const tb = $('sale-items'); if (!tb) return; let tq = 0, ta = 0;
  qsa('tr', tb).forEach(tr => {
    const q = pf(qs('[data-f="qty"]', tr) ? qs('[data-f="qty"]', tr).value : 0);
    const a = Math.round(q * saleRowEffRate(tr) * 100) / 100;
    const amt = qs('[data-amt]', tr); if (amt) amt.textContent = fmtNum(a);
    tq += q; ta += a;
  });
  if ($('sale-tqty')) $('sale-tqty').textContent = fmtNum(tq);
  if ($('sale-tamt')) $('sale-tamt').textContent = fmtINR(ta);
  renumberSr($('sale-items'));
}
/* Excel paste into the Sale grid: rows × the 21 columns, header row auto-skipped */
function saveSale(id) {
  clearReqMarks();
  const prevItems = (id && db.sale.find(r => r.id === id)) ? (db.sale.find(r => r.id === id).items || []) : null;
  const items = gatherRows('sale-items', ['desc', 'sku', 'styleCode', 'program', 'orderNo', 'orderLine', 'myntraSku', 'skuId']).map(function (i, idx) {
    i.amount = Math.round(pf(i.qty) * (pf(i.rate) > 0 ? pf(i.rate) : (pf(i.mrp) - pf(i.discount)) > 0 ? pf(i.mrp) - pf(i.discount) : pf(i.sp)) * 100) / 100;
    const prev = prevItems && prevItems[idx];
    if (prev) { if (prev.raw && !i.raw) i.raw = prev.raw; if (prev.rowNo != null && i.rowNo == null) i.rowNo = prev.rowNo; if (prev.imported && i.imported == null) i.imported = true; if (prev.state && !i.state) i.state = prev.state; if (prev.sellerId && !i.sellerId) i.sellerId = prev.sellerId; if (prev.program && !i.program) i.program = prev.program; }
    return i;
  });
  if (!items.length) return reqBadCell('sale-items', 0, 'desc', 'Add at least one sale item');
  const prevRec = id ? db.sale.find(r => r.id === id) : null;
  const data = {
    date: dval('f-date') || today(),
    customer: (prevRec && prevRec.customer) || 'Myntra D2C',
    channel: (prevRec && prevRec.channel) || 'Myntra D2C',
    payStatus: (prevRec && prevRec.payStatus) || 'Unpaid',
    payMode: (prevRec && prevRec.payMode) || '',
    invoiceNo: (prevRec && prevRec.invoiceNo) || '',
    notes: $('f-notes').value.trim(), items, status: cboxGet('sl-status')
  };
  if (id) Object.assign(db.sale.find(r => r.id === id), data);
  else { data.id = uid(); data.no = nextNo('sale','SL'); data.createdAt = Date.now(); db.sale.push(data); }
  saveDB(); pageView = null; toast('Sale saved', 'green'); render();
}

/* ---- the masters rail labels the export buttons use (User Management itself is gone) ---- */

function userMasterLabel(k){if(k==='companydetails')return 'Company Details';if(k==='loginpage')return 'Login Page';if(k==='menumaster')return 'Menu Management';const d=mastersDefs().find(function(x){return x.k===k;});return d?d.title:k;}

/* ---- the default window the Sale date range opens on ---- */
function defaultFrom() { const x = new Date(); x.setDate(x.getDate() - 30); return x.toISOString().slice(0, 10); }

function rdpAddDays(iso, n) {
  const d = dpParse(iso) || new Date();
  d.setDate(d.getDate() + n);
  return dpISO(d);
}
function rdpAddMonths(iso, n) {
  const d = dpParse(iso) || new Date();
  const day = d.getDate();
  d.setDate(1);
  d.setMonth(d.getMonth() + n);
  const max = new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth() + 1, 0).getDate();
  d.setDate(Math.min(day, max));
  return dpISO(d);
}
function rdpYesterday() { return rdpAddDays(today(), -1); }
/* week-to-date and previous-period windows, day granular */
function rdpSpecialRange(p) {
  const now = new Date(); now.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  const y = now.getFullYear();
  if (p === 'wtd') {
    const s = new Date(now); s.setDate(s.getDate() - now.getDay());
    return { from: dpISO(s), to: dpISO(now) };
  }
  if (p === 'lastweek') {
    const e = new Date(now); e.setDate(e.getDate() - now.getDay() - 1);
    const st = new Date(e); st.setDate(st.getDate() - 6);
    return { from: dpISO(st), to: dpISO(e) };
  }
  if (p === 'lastquarter') {
    const st = new Date(y, Math.floor(now.getMonth() / 3) * 3 - 3, 1);
    return { from: dpISO(st), to: dpISO(new Date(st.getFullYear(), st.getMonth() + 3, 0)) };
  }
  if (p === 'lastyear') return { from: (y - 1) + '-01-01', to: (y - 1) + '-12-31' };
  return null;
}
function rdpQuarterRange(p) {
  let y = new Date().getFullYear(), n = -1;
  const m = /^q(\d{4})-([1-4])$/.exec(p);
  if (m) { y = parseInt(m[1], 10); n = parseInt(m[2], 10) - 1; }
  else { const m2 = /^q([1-4])$/.exec(p); if (m2) n = parseInt(m2[1], 10) - 1; }
  if (n < 0) return null;
  return { from: dpISO(new Date(y, n * 3, 1)), to: dpISO(new Date(y, n * 3 + 3, 0)) };
}
/* newest finished quarters first, e.g. Q2 2026, Q1 2026, Q4 2025 ... */
function rdpRecentQuarters(count) {
  const out = [], now = new Date();
  let y = now.getFullYear(), q = Math.floor(now.getMonth() / 3); /* 0 = Q1; current quarter skipped */
  for (let i = 0; i < (count || 8); i++) {
    if (q === 0) { q = 3; y--; } else { q--; }
    out.push({ k: 'q' + y + '-' + (q + 1), l: 'Q' + (q + 1) + ' ' + y });
  }
  return out;
}
function rdpSyncState(r) {
  if (!r.preset) r.preset = 'last';
  if (!r.lastN) r.lastN = 30;
  if (!r.lastUnit) r.lastUnit = 'days';
  if (r.includeToday == null) r.includeToday = true;
  if (r.preset !== 'custom' && r.preset !== 'all' && (!r.from || !r.to)) {
    const x = rdpFromPreset(r);
    r.from = x.from; r.to = x.to;
  }
}
function rdpFromPreset(d) {
  d = d || {};
  const inc = d.includeToday !== false;
  const end = inc ? today() : rdpYesterday();
  const p = d.preset || 'last';
  if (p === 'all') return { from: '', to: '' };
  if (p === 'today') return { from: today(), to: today() };
  if (p === 'last30m' || p === 'last12h') return { from: today(), to: today() };
  if (p === 'yesterday') return { from: rdpYesterday(), to: rdpYesterday() };
  if (p === 'last') {
    const n = Math.max(1, parseInt(d.lastN, 10) || 30);
    const u = d.lastUnit || 'days';
    const from = u === 'weeks' ? rdpAddDays(end, -(n * 7)) : (u === 'months' ? rdpAddMonths(end, -n) : rdpAddDays(end, -n));
    return { from: from, to: end };
  }
  if (p === 'mtd' || p === 'ptd') {
    const t = dpParse(end);
    return { from: dpISO(new Date(t.getFullYear(), t.getMonth(), 1)), to: end };
  }
  if (p === 'qtd') {
    const t = dpParse(end);
    const q = Math.floor(t.getMonth() / 3) * 3;
    return { from: dpISO(new Date(t.getFullYear(), q, 1)), to: end };
  }
  if (p === 'ytd') {
    const t = dpParse(end);
    return { from: dpISO(new Date(t.getFullYear(), 0, 1)), to: end };
  }
  if (p === 'wtd' || p === 'lastweek' || p === 'lastquarter' || p === 'lastyear') {
    const sp = rdpSpecialRange(p);
    if (sp) return sp;
  }
  if (p === 'thisMonth') {
    const t = new Date();
    return { from: dpISO(new Date(t.getFullYear(), t.getMonth(), 1)), to: dpISO(new Date(t.getFullYear(), t.getMonth() + 1, 0)) };
  }
  if (p === 'lastMonth') {
    const t = new Date();
    return { from: dpISO(new Date(t.getFullYear(), t.getMonth() - 1, 1)), to: dpISO(new Date(t.getFullYear(), t.getMonth(), 0)) };
  }
  if (/^q(\d{4}-)?[1-4]$/.test(p)) {
    const qq = rdpQuarterRange(p);
    if (qq) return qq;
  }
  return { from: d.from || defaultFrom(), to: d.to || today() };
}

function rdpPrettyLong(iso) {
  const d = dpParse(iso); if (!d) return '';
  return DP_MON[d.getMonth()] + ' ' + d.getDate() + ', ' + d.getFullYear();
}
function rdpParseTyped(str) {
  const raw = String(str == null ? '' : str).trim();
  if (!/^\d{2}-\d{2}-\d{4}$/.test(raw)) return '';
  const iso = dpToISO(raw);
  const d = dpParse(iso);
  if (!d) return '';
  if (dpToDisplay(iso) !== raw) return '';
  return iso;
}
function rdpMaskDmy(el) {
  if (!el) return;
  const start = el.selectionStart;
  const old = String(el.value || '');
  let digits = old.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 8);
  let out = digits;
  if (digits.length > 4) out = digits.slice(0, 2) + '-' + digits.slice(2, 4) + '-' + digits.slice(4);
  else if (digits.length > 2) out = digits.slice(0, 2) + '-' + digits.slice(2);
  if (out !== old) {
    el.value = out;
    try {
      let pos = out.length;
      if (typeof start === 'number') {
        const before = old.slice(0, start).replace(/\D/g, '').length;
        if (before <= 2) pos = before;
        else if (before <= 4) pos = before + 1;
        else pos = before + 2;
      }
      el.setSelectionRange(pos, pos);
    } catch (e) {}
  }
}
function rdpCommitTyped(which, val, silent) {
  if (!RDP.draft) return false;
  const iso = rdpParseTyped(val);
  const inp = which === 'from' ? $('rdpFromInp') : $('rdpToInp');
  if (!iso) {
    if (inp) inp.value = dpToDisplay(RDP.draft[which]);
    if (String(val || '').trim()) toast('Enter date as DD-MM-YYYY', 'red');
    return false;
  }
  /* the boxes are painted in every state now, so Apply reads them; an untouched box
     must not demote a picked preset, only a value the user actually changed does */
  if (RDP.draft[which] === iso) return false;   /* a box that still says what the draft says is not an edit */
  RDP.nav = 'custom';
  RDP.draft.preset = 'custom';
  const same = RDP.draft[which] === iso;
  RDP.draft[which] = iso;
  if (which === 'from' && RDP.draft.to && iso > RDP.draft.to) RDP.draft.to = iso;
  if (which === 'to' && RDP.draft.from && iso < RDP.draft.from) RDP.draft.from = iso;
  const end = dpParse(iso);
  if (end) RDP.view = new Date(end.getFullYear(), end.getMonth(), 1);
  if (silent) {
    if (inp) inp.value = dpToDisplay(iso);
    return true;
  }
  if (same) { if (inp) inp.value = dpToDisplay(iso); return true; }
  rdpPaint();
  const again = which === 'from' ? $('rdpFromInp') : $('rdpToInp');
  if (again) { try { again.focus(); } catch (e) {} }
  return true;
}
function rdpRangeLabel(from, to) {
  const a = dpParse(from), b = dpParse(to);
  if (!a || !b) return 'All dates';
  const mon = ['Jan','Feb','Mar','Apr','May','Jun','Jul','Aug','Sep','Oct','Nov','Dec'];
  const sameDay = from === to;
  const sameMon = a.getMonth() === b.getMonth() && a.getFullYear() === b.getFullYear();
  const sameYr = a.getFullYear() === b.getFullYear();
  if (sameDay) return mon[a.getMonth()] + ' ' + a.getDate() + ', ' + a.getFullYear();
  if (sameMon) return mon[a.getMonth()] + ' ' + a.getDate() + '\u2013' + b.getDate() + ', ' + a.getFullYear();
  if (sameYr) return mon[a.getMonth()] + ' ' + a.getDate() + '\u2013' + mon[b.getMonth()] + ' ' + b.getDate() + ', ' + a.getFullYear();
  return mon[a.getMonth()] + ' ' + a.getDate() + ', ' + a.getFullYear() + '\u2013' + mon[b.getMonth()] + ' ' + b.getDate() + ', ' + b.getFullYear();
}
function rdpLabel(r) {
  r = r || SDATE;
  const p = r.preset || 'last';
  if (p === 'all') return 'All data';
  if (p === 'today') return 'Today';
  if (p === 'last30m') return 'Last 30 minutes';
  if (p === 'last12h') return 'Last 12 hours';
  if (p === 'wtd') return 'Week to date';
  if (p === 'lastweek') return 'Last week';
  if (p === 'lastquarter') return 'Last quarter';
  if (p === 'last12months') return 'Last 12 months';
  if (p === 'lastyear') return 'Last year';
  const qmx = /^q(\d{4})-([1-4])$/.exec(p);
  if (qmx) return 'Q' + qmx[2] + ' ' + qmx[1];
  if (p === 'yesterday') return 'Yesterday';
  if (p === 'last') {
    const n = r.lastN || 30;
    const u = r.lastUnit === 'weeks' ? (n === 1 ? 'week' : 'weeks') : (r.lastUnit === 'months' ? (n === 1 ? 'month' : 'months') : (n === 1 ? 'day' : 'days'));
    return 'Last ' + n + ' ' + u;
  }
  if (p === 'mtd' || p === 'ptd') return 'Month to date';
  if (p === 'qtd') return 'Quarter to date';
  if (p === 'ytd') return 'Year to date';
  if (p === 'thisMonth') return 'This month';
  if (p === 'lastMonth') return 'Last month';
  if (p === 'q1') return 'Q1';
  if (p === 'q2') return 'Q2';
  if (p === 'q3') return 'Q3';
  if (p === 'q4') return 'Q4';
  return 'Custom range';
}
function rdpTriggerHtml(anchorId, chipClears) {
  const a = anchorId || 'sfTrig';
  const r = SDATE;
  rdpSyncState(r);
  return '<button type="button" class="rdp-trig" id="' + a + '" onclick="rdpToggle(event,\'' + a + '\')' + '">' +
    '<span class="rdp-calic"><svg class="i" viewBox="0 0 20 20" width="16" height="16" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round"><rect x="3.25" y="4.75" width="13.5" height="12" rx="2"/><path d="M3.25 8.25h13.5M7 3.5v2.5M13 3.5v2.5"/></svg></span><span>' + esc(rdpLabel(r)) + '</span>' + ic('chevron', 14) +
    '</button>' + rdpChipHtml(r, chipClears);
}
/* the range read-back; when a window is on it is also the only way off it */
function rdpChipHtml(r, chipClears) {
  const label = esc(r.from && r.to ? rdpRangeLabel(r.from, r.to) : 'All dates');
  if (chipClears && r.from && r.to)
    return '<button type="button" class="rdp-chip rdp-chip-x" onclick="sfClear()" title="Clear the date window">' +
      label + '<span class="rdp-chip-xg" aria-hidden="true">\u00d7</span></button>';
  return '<span class="rdp-chip">' + label + '</span>';
}

const RDP_NAV = [
  { k:'today', l:'Today' },
  { k:'yesterday', l:'Yesterday' },
  { k:'last', l:'Last' },
  { k:'ptd', l:'Period to date' },
  { k:'quarters', l:'Quarters' },
  { k:'custom', l:'Custom range' }
];
/* the rail has no `All data` row: the view starts on everything, and the range chip in the
   toolbar is how you get back to it */
let RDP = { open:false, el:null, nav:'last', grp:false, draft:null, view:null, pick:null, focus:null };
let RDP_ANCHOR = 'sfTrig';
/* one shared date window for the Sale list page, both dashboards and the export */
let SDATE = { from:'', to:'', preset:'all', lastN:30, lastUnit:'days', includeToday:true };
function sfActive(){ return !!(SDATE.from && SDATE.to); }
/* a sale document stays when any of its lines falls inside the window */
function rdpNavOf(preset) {
  const p = preset || 'last';
  if (p === 'all' || p === 'today' || p === 'yesterday' || p === 'custom') return p;
  if (p === 'last' || /^last\d+d$/.test(p) || /^last\d+months$/.test(p) ||
      p === 'last30m' || p === 'last12h' || p === 'lastweek' || p === 'lastMonth' || p === 'thisMonth' ||
      p === 'lastquarter' || p === 'last12months' || p === 'lastyear') return 'last';
  if (p === 'wtd' || p === 'mtd' || p === 'qtd' || p === 'ytd' || p === 'ptd') return 'ptd';
  if (/^q(\d{4}-)?[1-4]$/.test(p)) return 'quarters';
  return 'custom';
}
/* Shopify Analytics-style rail: groups hold the long lists, opened in place */
/* no sub-day rows: every ledger here is date granular, so `Last 30 minutes` would
   silently mean `today`. The codes still resolve, for state saved earlier. */
const RDP_LAST_ITEMS = [
  ['7d', 'Last 7 days'], ['30d', 'Last 30 days'], ['90d', 'Last 90 days'], ['365d', 'Last 365 days'],
  ['lastweek', 'Last week'], ['lastMonth', 'Last month'], ['lastquarter', 'Last quarter'],
  ['12months', 'Last 12 months'], ['lastyear', 'Last year']
];
const RDP_GRP_NAVS = { last:1, ptd:1, quarters:1 };   /* these three swap their list into the rail */
const RDP_PTD_ITEMS = [['wtd', 'Week to date'], ['mtd', 'Month to date'], ['qtd', 'Quarter to date'], ['ytd', 'Year to date']];
/** the rows a group owns, with the block breaks marked and the lit one named */
function rdpGroupOf(nav, d) {
  if (nav === 'last') {
    const lkey = rdpLastKey(d);
    return { lit: lkey, items: RDP_LAST_ITEMS.map(function (x) {
      return { k: x[0], l: x[1], gap: x[0] === 'lastweek' };
    }) };
  }
  if (nav === 'ptd') {
    return { lit: d.preset, items: RDP_PTD_ITEMS.map(function (x) { return { k: x[0], l: x[1], gap: false }; }) };
  }
  if (nav === 'quarters') {
    /* a bare `q1` in the state means this year's Q1, which the list writes as `q2026-1` */
    let lit = d.preset;
    const bare = /^q([1-4])$/.exec(lit || '');
    if (bare) { const y = new Date(); lit = 'q' + y.getFullYear() + '-' + bare[1]; }
    let prevY = '';
    return { lit: lit, items: rdpRecentQuarters(8).map(function (x) {
      const y = /^q(\d{4})-/.exec(x.k), yy = y ? y[1] : '';
      const gap = !!prevY && !!yy && yy !== prevY;
      if (yy) prevY = yy;
      return { k: x.k, l: x.l, gap: gap };
    }) };
  }
  return null;
}
function rdpEnsure() {
  if (RDP.el) return RDP.el;
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.className = 'rdp-pop';
  el.id = 'rdpPop';
  el.addEventListener('mousedown', function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); });
  document.body.appendChild(el);
  RDP.el = el;
  return el;
}
function rdpHide() {
  RDP.open = false;
  if (RDP.el) RDP.el.classList.remove('show');
}
function rdpToggle(e, anchorId) {
  if (e) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); }
  if (RDP.open && RDP_ANCHOR === (anchorId || 'sfTrig')) { rdpHide(); return; }
  rdpOpen(anchorId);
}
function rdpOpen(anchorId) {
  RDP_ANCHOR = anchorId || 'sfTrig';
  const r = SDATE;
  rdpSyncState(r);
  RDP.draft = {
    from: r.from, to: r.to, preset: r.preset || 'all',
    lastN: r.lastN || 30, lastUnit: r.lastUnit || 'days',
    includeToday: r.includeToday !== false
  };
  RDP.nav = rdpNavOf(RDP.draft.preset);
  RDP.grp = false;                       /* the list swaps in only on the click */
  const end = dpParse(RDP.draft.to) || new Date();
  RDP.view = new Date(end.getFullYear(), end.getMonth(), 1);
  RDP.pick = null;
  RDP.open = true;
  rdpPaint();
  const el = rdpEnsure();
  el.classList.add('show');
  const trig = $(RDP_ANCHOR);
  if (trig) {
    const box = trig.getBoundingClientRect();
    const w = Math.min(el.offsetWidth || 760, window.innerWidth - 16);
    let left = box.left;
    if (left + w > window.innerWidth - 8) left = Math.max(8, window.innerWidth - 8 - w);
    /* the box is only as tall as its content now, so measure it instead of trusting a
       reserved height - otherwise it flips upward for no reason on a short screen */
    const h = el.offsetHeight || 400;
    let top = box.bottom + 6;
    if (top + h > window.innerHeight - 8) top = Math.max(8, box.top - 6 - Math.min(h, window.innerHeight - 16));
    el.style.left = Math.round(left) + 'px';
    el.style.top = Math.round(top) + 'px';
  }
}
function rdpLastKey(d) {
  if (d.preset === 'last') {
    if (d.lastUnit === 'months' && d.lastN === 12) return '12months';
    if (d.lastUnit === 'days') return d.lastN + 'd';
    return '';
  }
  return d.preset || '';
}
function rdpApplyPreset(nav, preset) {
  RDP.nav = nav;
  RDP.grp = !!RDP_GRP_NAVS[nav];                 /* the group takes over the rail */
  if (preset === '12months') { RDP.draft.preset = 'last'; RDP.draft.lastN = 12; RDP.draft.lastUnit = 'months'; preset = ''; }
  if (preset === 'last30m' || preset === 'last12h') RDP.draft.includeToday = true;
  else if (/^\d+d$/.test(preset || '')) { RDP.draft.preset = 'last'; RDP.draft.lastN = parseInt(preset, 10); RDP.draft.lastUnit = 'days'; preset = ''; }
  if (preset) RDP.draft.preset = preset;
  else if (nav === 'last') RDP.draft.preset = 'last';
  else if (nav === 'ptd') RDP.draft.preset = RDP.draft.preset === 'qtd' || RDP.draft.preset === 'ytd' ? RDP.draft.preset : 'mtd';
  else if (nav === 'quarters') RDP.draft.preset = /^q[1-4]$/.test(RDP.draft.preset) ? RDP.draft.preset : 'q1';
  else if (nav === 'custom') { RDP.draft.preset = 'custom'; RDP.focus = 'from'; }
  else RDP.draft.preset = nav;
  if (RDP.draft.preset !== 'custom') {
    const x = rdpFromPreset(RDP.draft);
    RDP.draft.from = x.from; RDP.draft.to = x.to;
    RDP.pick = null;
    const end = dpParse(x.to) || new Date();
    RDP.view = new Date(end.getFullYear(), end.getMonth(), 1);
  }
  rdpPaint();
}
function rdpShift(n) {
  RDP.view.setMonth(RDP.view.getMonth() + n);
  rdpPaint();
}
function rdpPick(iso) {
  RDP.nav = 'custom';
  RDP.draft.preset = 'custom';
  const focus = RDP.focus || 'from';
  if (focus === 'to') {
    RDP.draft.to = iso;
    if (!RDP.draft.from || iso < RDP.draft.from) RDP.draft.from = iso;
  } else {
    RDP.draft.from = iso;
    if (!RDP.draft.to || iso > RDP.draft.to) RDP.draft.to = iso;
    RDP.focus = 'to';
  }
  const end = dpParse(iso);
  if (end) RDP.view = new Date(end.getFullYear(), end.getMonth(), 1);
  rdpPaint();
}
function rdpMonthHtml(anchor) {
  const y = anchor.getFullYear(), m = anchor.getMonth();
  const first = new Date(y, m, 1);
  const lead = first.getDay();
  const days = new Date(y, m + 1, 0).getDate();
  const from = RDP.draft.from, to = RDP.draft.to;
  const tIso = today();
  let cells = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < lead; i++) cells += '<span class="dpc dpc-mute"></span>';
  for (let d = 1; d <= days; d++) {
    const iso = dpISO(new Date(y, m, d));
    const inR = from && to && iso >= from && iso <= to;
    const sel = iso === from || iso === to;
    const cls = 'dpc' + (sel ? ' dpc-sel' : '') + (inR ? ' dpc-in' : '') + (iso === tIso ? ' dpc-today' : '');
    cells += '<button type="button" class="' + cls + '" data-rdp-day="' + iso + '">' + d + '</button>';
  }
  return '<div class="rdp-cal">' +
    '<div class="rdp-cal-h">' + DP_MON[m] + ' ' + y + '</div>' +
    '<div class="dpop-dow">' + ['Su','Mo','Tu','We','Th','Fr','Sa'].map(function (x) { return '<span>' + x + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
    '<div class="dpop-grid">' + cells + '</div></div>';
}
function rdpPaint() {
  const el = rdpEnsure();
  const d = RDP.draft;
  const navList = RDP_NAV;
  let side;
  const grp = RDP.grp ? rdpGroupOf(RDP.nav, d) : null;
  if (grp) {
    /* the group's own list, swapped into the rail: a back row, then its rows broken into
       blocks the way Shopify spaces them (fixed counts | calendar spans, quarters by year) */
    const g0 = navList.filter(function (x) { return x.k === RDP.nav; })[0];
    let rows = '<button type="button" class="rdp-si rdp-si-back" data-rdp-grp="0">' + ic('back', 14) + esc(g0 ? g0.l : 'Last') + '</button>';
    grp.items.forEach(function (x, n) {
      if (x.gap && n) rows += '<div class="rdp-si-gap"></div>';
      rows += '<button type="button" class="rdp-si' + (grp.lit === x.k ? ' on' : '') + '" data-rdp-pre="' + x.k + '">' + esc(x.l) + '</button>';
    });
    side = rows;
  } else {
    side = navList.map(function (x) {
      return '<button type="button" class="rdp-si' + (RDP.nav === x.k ? ' on' : '') + '" data-rdp-nav="' + x.k + '">' + esc(x.l) + '</button>';
    }).join('');
  }
  /* the resolved window sits above the months in every state, not only in Custom range:
     pick Last 90 days and the two boxes show what that means; typing into either one
     hands the popover over to the typed range (rdpCommitTyped flips nav to custom) */
  if (!RDP.focus) RDP.focus = 'from';
  const onCls = RDP.nav === 'custom' ? RDP.focus : '';
  const dates = '<div class="rdp-dates">' +
    '<div class="rdp-date' + (onCls === 'from' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-rdp-focus="from">' +
      '<input class="inp" id="rdpFromInp" data-rdp-focus="from" inputmode="numeric" maxlength="10" value="' + esc(dpToDisplay(d.from)) + '" placeholder="DD-MM-YYYY" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false">' +
    '</div>' +
    '<span class="rdp-arrow">\u2192</span>' +
    '<div class="rdp-date' + (onCls === 'to' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-rdp-focus="to">' +
      '<input class="inp" id="rdpToInp" data-rdp-focus="to" inputmode="numeric" maxlength="10" value="' + esc(dpToDisplay(d.to)) + '" placeholder="DD-MM-YYYY" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false">' +
    '</div>' +
    '</div>';
  const left = new Date(RDP.view.getFullYear(), RDP.view.getMonth(), 1);
  const right = new Date(RDP.view.getFullYear(), RDP.view.getMonth() + 1, 1);
  el.innerHTML =
    '<div class="rdp-side">' + side + '</div>' +
    '<div class="rdp-main">' + dates +
      '<div class="rdp-cals">' +
        '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center">' +
          '<button type="button" class="dpop-nav" data-rdp-shift="-1" aria-label="Previous month">' + ic('back', 16) + '</button></div>' +
        rdpMonthHtml(left) + rdpMonthHtml(right) +
        '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center">' +
          '<button type="button" class="dpop-nav dpop-next" data-rdp-shift="1" aria-label="Next month">' + ic('back', 16) + '</button></div>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<div class="rdp-foot"><span class="rdp-sum">' + esc(rdpRangeLabel(d.from, d.to)) + '</span>' +
        '<div style="display:flex;gap:8px">' +
          '<button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost" data-rdp-act="cancel">Cancel</button>' +
          '<button type="button" class="btn btn-primary" data-rdp-act="apply">Apply</button>' +
        '</div></div></div>';
  if (!el._bound) {
    el._bound = true;
    el.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      const foc = e.target.closest('[data-rdp-focus]');
      if (foc) {
        RDP.focus = foc.getAttribute('data-rdp-focus');
        qsa('.rdp-date', el).forEach(function (div) {
          div.classList.toggle('on', div.getAttribute('data-rdp-focus') === RDP.focus);
        });
        const inp = (e.target && e.target.matches && e.target.matches('input')) ? e.target : (RDP.focus === 'from' ? $('rdpFromInp') : $('rdpToInp'));
        if (inp) { try { inp.focus(); } catch (err) {} }
        return;
      }
      const gp = e.target.closest('[data-rdp-grp]');
      if (gp) { RDP.grp = false; rdpPaint(); return; }
      const nav = e.target.closest('[data-rdp-nav]');
      if (nav) { rdpApplyPreset(nav.getAttribute('data-rdp-nav')); return; }
      const pre = e.target.closest('[data-rdp-pre]');
      if (pre) { rdpApplyPreset(RDP.nav, pre.getAttribute('data-rdp-pre')); return; }
      const sh = e.target.closest('[data-rdp-shift]');
      if (sh) { rdpShift(+sh.getAttribute('data-rdp-shift')); return; }
      const day = e.target.closest('[data-rdp-day]');
      if (day) { rdpPick(day.getAttribute('data-rdp-day')); return; }
      const act = e.target.closest('[data-rdp-act]');
      if (act) {
        if (act.getAttribute('data-rdp-act') === 'apply') rdpApply();
        else rdpHide();
      }
    });
    el.addEventListener('mousedown', function (e) {
      const act = e.target.closest('[data-rdp-act]');
      if (act && act.getAttribute('data-rdp-act') === 'apply') {
        e.preventDefault();
        rdpApply();
      }
    });
    el.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
      if (e.target && e.target.id === 'rdpFromInp') rdpCommitTyped('from', e.target.value);
      if (e.target && e.target.id === 'rdpToInp') rdpCommitTyped('to', e.target.value);
    });
    el.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
      if (e.target && (e.target.id === 'rdpFromInp' || e.target.id === 'rdpToInp')) rdpMaskDmy(e.target);
    });
    el.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
      if (e.target && (e.target.id === 'rdpFromInp' || e.target.id === 'rdpToInp')) {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); rdpCommitTyped(e.target.id === 'rdpFromInp' ? 'from' : 'to', e.target.value); return; }
        if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;
        if (e.key && e.key.length === 1 && !/[0-9]/.test(e.key)) e.preventDefault();
      }
    });
  }
}
function rdpApply() {
  const fromInp = $('rdpFromInp'), toInp = $('rdpToInp');
  if (fromInp) rdpCommitTyped('from', fromInp.value, true);
  if (toInp) rdpCommitTyped('to', toInp.value, true);
  const d = RDP.draft; if (!d) return;
  if (d.preset === 'all') { d.from = ''; d.to = ''; }
  else if (d.from && d.to && d.from > d.to) { const t = d.from; d.from = d.to; d.to = t; }
  else if (!d.from || !d.to) return;
  const r = SDATE;
  r.from = d.from; r.to = d.to; r.preset = d.preset;
  r.lastN = d.lastN; r.lastUnit = d.lastUnit; r.includeToday = d.includeToday;
  rdpHide();
  render();
}
document.addEventListener('mousedown', function (e) {
  if (!RDP.open) return;
  const pop = RDP.el, trig = $(RDP_ANCHOR);
  if (pop && pop.contains(e.target)) return;
  if (trig && trig.contains(e.target)) return;
  rdpHide();
});
document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
  if (e.key === 'Escape' && RDP.open) { e.preventDefault(); rdpHide(); }
});

/* which columns hold numbers — so the header, the values and the totals row
   all line up on the same edge */



/* =====================================================================
   COSTING — buyer order costing (fabric + trims BOM, cost break-up, FOB)
   ===================================================================== */

/* Excel-style keyboard / paste / fill-handle — scoped to costing sheets */
/* =====================================================================
   GLOBAL ADMIN SEARCH — Shopify-style dropdown across the Sale workspace
   ===================================================================== */
const GB_MODS = [
  { k:'sale', label:'Sale' }
];
const gbState = { q:'', mod:'', limit:8, open:false, active:0, items:[] };

function gbMod(k) { return GB_MODS.find(function (x) { return x.k === k; }) || { k:k, label:k }; }

function gbHay(obj, depth) {
  if (obj == null) return '';
  if (typeof obj !== 'object') return String(obj);
  if ((depth || 0) > 3) return '';
  if (Array.isArray(obj)) return obj.map(function (x) { return gbHay(x, (depth || 0) + 1); }).join(' ');
  const skip = { pod:1, podName:1, styleImage:1, image:1, id:1, createdAt:1 };
  let s = '';
  Object.keys(obj).forEach(function (k) {
    if (skip[k]) return;
    const v = obj[k];
    if (v && typeof v === 'object') s += ' ' + gbHay(v, (depth || 0) + 1);
    else if (v != null && v !== '') s += ' ' + v;
  });
  return s;
}

function gbRecords(m) {
  
  
  return (db[m] || []).map(function (r) {
    const party = r.supplier || r.vendor || r.customer || r.party || '';
    let extra = '';
    if (m === 'sale' && r.channel) extra = r.channel;
    const amt = (m === 'sale') ? sumBy(r.items, 'amount') : 0;
    const sub = [party, extra, dpToDisplay(r.date) || r.date, amt ? fmtINR(amt) : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
    return {
      kind:'hit', m:m, id:r.id,
      title: r.no || '',
      sub: sub,
      status: r.status || '',
      date: r.date || '',
      hay: (gbHay(r) + ' ' + extra).toLowerCase()
    };
  });
}

function gbMatch(rec, words) {
  if (!words.length) return true;
  const hay = rec.hay || '';
  return words.every(function (w) { return hay.indexOf(w) > -1; });
}

function gbSuggestMods(q) {
  const qq = String(q || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  return GB_MODS.filter(function (x) {
    if (gbState.mod && x.k === gbState.mod) return false;
    if (!qq) return true;
    return x.label.toLowerCase().indexOf(qq) > -1 || x.k.indexOf(qq) > -1;
  });
}

function gbCollect() {
  const words = gbState.q.toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  const mods = gbState.mod ? GB_MODS.filter(function (x) { return x.k === gbState.mod; }) : GB_MODS;
  const hits = [];
  mods.forEach(function (src) {
    gbRecords(src.k).forEach(function (rec) {
      if (gbMatch(rec, words)) hits.push(rec);
    });
  });
  hits.sort(function (a, b) {
    const da = a.date || '', dbv = b.date || '';
    if (da !== dbv) return dbv < da ? -1 : 1;
    return String(b.title || '').localeCompare(String(a.title || ''));
  });
  return hits;
}

function gbRenderChip() {
  const el = $('gbChips'); if (!el) return;
  if (!gbState.mod) { el.innerHTML = ''; return; }
  el.innerHTML = '<span class="gb-chip">' + esc(gbMod(gbState.mod).label) +
    '<button type="button" class="gb-chip-x" title="Remove filter" onclick="gbClearMod(event)">' + ic('x', 12) + '</button></span>';
}

function gbClearMod(e) {
  if (e) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); }
  gbState.mod = '';
  gbState.limit = 8;
  gbState.active = 0;
  gbRenderChip();
  gbShow();
  const inp = $('gbSearch'); if (inp) inp.focus();
}

function gbSetMod(k) {
  gbState.mod = k;
  gbState.limit = 8;
  gbState.active = 0;
  gbRenderChip();
  gbShow();
  const inp = $('gbSearch'); if (inp) inp.focus();
}

function gbHide() {
  gbState.open = false;
  const pop = $('gbPop'); if (pop) pop.classList.remove('show');
  const inp = $('gbSearch'); if (inp) inp.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
}

function gbOpenHit(hit) {
  gbHide();
  const inp = $('gbSearch');
  if (inp) { inp.value = ''; }
  gbState.q = '';
  gbState.mod = '';
  gbState.limit = 8;
  gbRenderChip();
  if (!hit) return;
  
  
  cur = hit.m;
  pageView = { kind:'form', m: hit.m, id: hit.id };
  if (typeof isMobileNav === 'function' && isMobileNav()) toggleNav(false);
  render();
}

function gbPaint() {
  const pop = $('gbPop'); if (!pop) return;
  const q = gbState.q.trim();
  const items = [];
  if (!gbState.mod) {
    gbSuggestMods(q).forEach(function (x) {
      items.push({ kind:'mod', m:x.k, title:x.label, sub:'Search in ' + x.label });
    });
  }
  let hits = (q || gbState.mod) ? gbCollect() : [];
  const shown = hits.slice(0, gbState.limit);
  shown.forEach(function (h) { items.push(h); });
  const remain = hits.length - shown.length;
  if (remain > 0) items.push({ kind:'more', more: remain });
  gbState.items = items;
  if (gbState.active >= items.length) gbState.active = items.length ? items.length - 1 : 0;

  let html = '';
  let i = 0;
  const mods = items.filter(function (x) { return x.kind === 'mod'; });
  const recs = items.filter(function (x) { return x.kind === 'hit'; });
  if (mods.length) {
    html += '<div class="gb-sec">' + (q ? 'Filter to' : 'Search in') + '</div>';
    mods.forEach(function (it) {
      const idx = i++;
      html += '<button type="button" class="gb-row' + (gbState.active === idx ? ' on' : '') + '" data-gb="' + idx + '" role="option">' +
        '<span class="gb-ico">' + ic((MODULES[it.m] || {}).icon || 'search', 16) + '</span>' +
        '<span class="gb-body"><span class="gb-t">' + esc(it.title) + '</span>' +
        '<div class="gb-s">' + esc(it.sub) + '</div></span></button>';
    });
  }
  if (recs.length) {
    html += '<div class="gb-sec">' + (gbState.mod ? esc(gbMod(gbState.mod).label) : 'Records') +
      (hits.length ? ' · ' + hits.length : '') + '</div>';
    recs.forEach(function (it) {
      const idx = i++;
      const st = it.status ? badge(it.status) : '';
      const modLab = gbState.mod ? '' : '<span class="badge b-gray">' + esc(gbMod(it.m).label) + '</span>';
      html += '<button type="button" class="gb-row' + (gbState.active === idx ? ' on' : '') + '" data-gb="' + idx + '" role="option">' +
        '<span class="gb-ico">' + ic((MODULES[it.m] || {}).icon || 'order', 16) + '</span>' +
        '<span class="gb-body"><span class="gb-t">' + esc(it.title) + modLab + st + '</span>' +
        (it.sub ? '<div class="gb-s">' + esc(it.sub) + '</div>' : '') + '</span></button>';
    });
  }
  if (remain > 0) {
    const idx = i++;
    html += '<button type="button" class="gb-more' + (gbState.active === idx ? ' on' : '') + '" data-gb="' + idx + '">Show ' + Math.min(20, remain) + ' more</button>';
  }
  if (!html) {
    html = '<div class="gb-empty">' + (q ? 'No matches for “' + esc(q) + '”' : 'Type to search Sale records, sale numbers and order numbers') + '</div>';
  }
  pop.innerHTML = html;
  const on = pop.querySelector('[data-gb="' + gbState.active + '"]');
  if (on && on.scrollIntoView) on.scrollIntoView({ block:'nearest' });
}

function gbShow() {
  gbState.open = true;
  gbPaint();
  const pop = $('gbPop'); if (pop) pop.classList.add('show');
  const inp = $('gbSearch'); if (inp) inp.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
}

function gbActivate(dir) {
  const n = gbState.items.length;
  if (!n) return;
  gbState.active = (gbState.active + dir + n) % n;
  gbPaint();
}

function gbPickMod(it) {
  const lab = gbMod(it.m).label.toLowerCase();
  const qq = gbState.q.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (qq === lab || qq === it.m) {
    gbState.q = '';
    const inp = $('gbSearch'); if (inp) inp.value = '';
  }
  gbSetMod(it.m);
}

function gbGoActive() {
  const it = gbState.items[gbState.active];
  if (!it) return;
  if (it.kind === 'more') { gbState.limit += 20; gbState.active = 0; gbShow(); return; }
  if (it.kind === 'mod') { gbPickMod(it); return; }
  gbOpenHit(it);
}

function bindGbSearch() {
  const inp = $('gbSearch'); if (!inp || inp._gbBound) return;
  inp._gbBound = true;
  inp.addEventListener('focus', function () { gbShow(); });
  inp.addEventListener('input', function () {
    gbState.q = inp.value;
    gbState.limit = 8;
    gbState.active = 0;
    gbShow();
  });
  inp.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') { e.preventDefault(); if (!gbState.open) gbShow(); else gbActivate(1); }
    else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') { e.preventDefault(); gbActivate(-1); }
    else if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); gbGoActive(); }
    else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (gbState.mod && !String(inp.value || '').trim()) gbClearMod();
      else if (gbState.open) gbHide();
      else inp.blur();
    } else if (e.key === 'Backspace' && !inp.value && gbState.mod) {
      gbClearMod();
    }
  });
  const pop = $('gbPop');
  if (pop) pop.addEventListener('mousedown', function (e) {
    const row = e.target.closest('[data-gb]');
    if (!row) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    gbState.active = +row.getAttribute('data-gb');
    gbGoActive();
  });
  document.addEventListener('mousedown', function (e) {
    const wrap = $('gbSearchWrap');
    if (wrap && !wrap.contains(e.target)) gbHide();
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'k') {
      e.preventDefault();
      inp.focus();
      try { inp.select(); } catch (err) {}
      gbShow();
    }
  });
}

/* ---------------- init ---------------- */
try {
  applyNav();
  /* a signed-in visitor lands on Sale Numbers, exactly as the sign-in hand-off does;
     render() on its own would stop one view short, on the Sale ledger */
  if (authUser() && userCan('sale','view')) openSaleNumbers(); else render();
  bindGbSearch();
  var lm = document.getElementById('loadMsg'); if (lm) lm.style.display = 'none';
} catch (e) {
  showErr('Startup error: ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : e), e && e.stack);
}
</script>
<script>
/* StitchFlow QA remediation layer — browser-only edition (no server/database changes). */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  const QA_DB_KEY = typeof DB_KEY !== 'undefined' ? DB_KEY : 'stitchflow_erp_v2';
  let qaDirty = false, qaApplyingRemote = false, qaUiTimer = 0;

  function qaArrayMerge(localArr, diskArr) {
    const out = (localArr || []).slice(), ids = new Set(out.map(x => x && x.id).filter(Boolean));
    (diskArr || []).forEach(function (x) { if (x && x.id && !ids.has(x.id)) { out.push(x); ids.add(x.id); } });
    return out;
  }
  function qaMergeUnseen(disk) {
    if (!disk || typeof disk !== 'object') return;
    /* the demo dataset is gone for good: a save arriving from an older tab must not bring it back */
    const SF_DEMO_ROW = /^Demo\s*\u2014/;
    ['sale','auditTrail','customReports'].forEach(function (k) {
      const inc = (k === 'sale' && Array.isArray(disk.sale))
        ? disk.sale.filter(function (r) { return !SF_DEMO_ROW.test(String((r && r.notes) || '')); })
        : disk[k];
      db[k] = qaArrayMerge(db[k], inc);
    });
    db.seq = db.seq || {};
    Object.keys(disk.seq || {}).forEach(function (k) { db.seq[k] = Math.max(pf(db.seq[k]), pf(disk.seq[k])); });
  }

  saveDB = function () {
    try { captureAuditChanges(); } catch (e) { console.warn('Audit capture failed', e); }
    try {
      // v5: only pull in records from disk that are NEWER than the in-memory
      // copy (cross-tab sync). Without this, every save re-imports the
      // previous disk version and silently undoes local deletes (including
      // Clear all). The fresh in-memory copy is stamped with a current
      // timestamp BEFORE we read disk, so disk can never be "newer" than a
      // freshly-rebuilt db.
      db._revision = Date.now();
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(QA_DB_KEY);
      if (raw) {
        try {
          const disk = JSON.parse(raw);
          if (pf(disk._revision) > pf(db._revision)) {
            qaMergeUnseen(disk);
          }
        } catch (_) { /* ignore parse errors */ }
      }
      const payload = JSON.stringify(db);
      localStorage.setItem(QA_DB_KEY, payload);
      if (localStorage.getItem(QA_DB_KEY) !== payload) throw new Error('Saved value could not be verified');
      try { localStorage.setItem(QA_DB_KEY + '_backup', payload); } catch (_) {}
      useLS = true; window.__qaSaveFailed = false; qaDirty = false; qaClearDraft();
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      useLS = false; window.__qaSaveFailed = true;
      toast('Not saved — browser storage is full or unavailable. Keep this form open and export/clear space.', 'red');
      return false;
    }
  };

  const qaToast = toast;
  toast = function (msg, type) {
    if (window.__qaSaveFailed && type === 'green' && /saved|updated|created|changed/i.test(String(msg || ''))) {
      window.__qaSaveFailed = false;
      return qaToast('Not saved — please keep the form open and retry after freeing browser storage.', 'red');
    }
    return qaToast(msg, type);
  };

  window.addEventListener('storage', function (e) {
    if (e.key !== QA_DB_KEY || !e.newValue || qaApplyingRemote || qaDirty) return;
    try {
      qaApplyingRemote = true;
      const incoming = JSON.parse(e.newValue);
      qaMergeUnseen(incoming);
      if (typeof render === 'function' && !$('authLogin')) render();
      toast('Changes from another tab were loaded', 'green');
    } catch (_) {} finally { qaApplyingRemote = false; }
  });

  function qaStyleKey(v) { return String(v || '').split(' · ')[0].trim().toLowerCase(); }
  window.qaStyleKey = qaStyleKey;

  const qaGbCollect=gbCollect;
  gbCollect=function(){const rows=qaGbCollect(),q=String(gbState.q||'').trim().toLowerCase();return rows.sort(function(a,b){const ae=String(a.title||'').toLowerCase()===q?0:1,be=String(b.title||'').toLowerCase()===q?0:1;return ae-be;});};

  const qaUserCan=userCan;
  userCan=function(m,action){
    if(!MODULES[m])return false;   /* same rule here: no page behind a removed module to grant */
    const u=authUser(); if(!u)return false; if(u.role==='Admin')return true;
    action=action||'view';
    if(['export','print','approve','close'].includes(action)){
      const p=(((u.permissions||{}).modules||{})[m]||{}); return !!p[action];
    }
    return qaUserCan(m,action);
  };

  function qaNotifStoreKey(){
    const u=authUser();
    return 'stitchflow_notif_read_' + ((u&&u.id)||'anon');
  }
  function qaNotifReadAt(){
    try{return parseInt(localStorage.getItem(qaNotifStoreKey())||'0',10)||0;}catch(e){return 0;}
  }
  function qaMarkNotifsRead(){
    try{localStorage.setItem(qaNotifStoreKey(), String(Date.now()));}catch(e){}
    qaRefreshBellBadge();
  }
  /* the approval feed used to pull live PO rows in; with only Sale left the bell
     reads the stored notification list and nothing else */
  function qaPoNotifList(){ return qaEnsureNotifs().slice(0, 40); }
  function qaEnsureNotifs(){
    if(!db.notifications) db.notifications=[];
    return db.notifications;
  }
  function qaRefreshBellBadge(){
    const bell=qs('.globalbar .gb-btn'); if(!bell) return;
    let badge=bell.querySelector('.gb-badge');
    const readAt=qaNotifReadAt();
    const n=qaPoNotifList().filter(function(x){return (x.at||0)>readAt;}).length;
    if(!n){ if(badge) badge.remove(); return; }
    if(!badge){ badge=document.createElement('span'); badge.className='gb-badge'; bell.appendChild(badge); }
    badge.textContent=n>99?'99+':String(n);
  }
  function qaCanApprove(module){
    const u=authUser();if(!u)return false;
    if(u.role==='Admin')return true;
    if(u.role==='Store' || u.role==='Production' || u.role==='Accounts') return false;
    if(u.role==='Manager'){
      const mgrModules=['sale'];
      return mgrModules.indexOf(module)>-1;
    }
    const p=((u.permissions||{}).modules||{})[module]||{};
    return !!p.approve;
  }
  window.qaCanApprove = qaCanApprove;
  window.qaSetStatus=function(module,id,status){
    const r=(db[module]||[]).find(x=>x.id===id);
    if(!r)return;
    const isApprover = qaCanApprove(module);
    // Submit (Draft → Submitted) is allowed for any user who can edit the record
    const isSubmit = status==='Submitted' && (r.status==='Draft' || r.status==='Rejected');
    if(!isApprover && !isSubmit){
      // Allow Close/Reopen/Cancel if user can edit (existing record edit rights)
      if(!userCanRecord(module, r, 'edit')) return toast('You do not have permission to change this status','red');
      // For non-approver non-submit cases, allow only if the new status is already in workflow
      const allowedNonApprover = ['Draft','Closed','Cancelled'];
      if(allowedNonApprover.indexOf(status) < 0) return toast('Approve permission is required to change to ' + status,'red');
    }
    // v5: if rejecting, require a mandatory reason (only approvers can reject)
    if(status==='Rejected' && !r._qaAskingReject){
      openDialog({
        title:'Reject ' + module.toUpperCase() + ' — ' + (r.no || ''),
        message:'Provide a reason for rejection. This reason is mandatory and will be recorded on the document.',
        bodyHtml:'<div class="dlg-field"><label>Reason for rejection *</label><textarea class="inp" id="qa-reject-reason" rows="3" placeholder="e.g. Price too high, wrong specs, duplicate PO, etc."></textarea></div>',
        confirmText:'Reject '+module.toUpperCase(),
        danger:true,
        onConfirm:function(){
          const reason=(($('qa-reject-reason')||{}).value||'').trim();
          if(!reason){ reqBad('qa-reject-reason','Rejection reason is required'); return false; }
          r.rejectReason=reason;
          r.rejectedAt=Date.now();
          r.rejectedBy=(authUser()&&(authUser().name||authUser().username))||'unknown';
          r._qaAskingReject=false;
          // apply status directly (avoid recursive dialog)
          r.status=status;
          r.updatedAt=Date.now();
          auditPush(module,'Status changed',r.no,['Status → Rejected (Reason: ' + reason + ')']);
          if(saveDB()){ toast(r.no+' rejected','green'); render(); }
        },
        onCancel:function(){ r._qaAskingReject=false; },
        after:function(){ const ta=$('qa-reject-reason'); if(ta) ta.focus(); r._qaAskingReject=true; }
      });
      return;
    }
    // v5: cancellation needs a reason too (for audit). A plain re-open does not.
    if(status==='Cancelled' && r.status!=='Cancelled' && !r._qaAskingCancel){
      openDialog({
        title:'Cancel ' + module.toUpperCase() + ' — ' + (r.no || ''),
        message:'Cancelling a record keeps it in the system for audit but excludes it from active reports and stock. <br><br>A reason is required and will be recorded on the document.',
        bodyHtml:'<div class="dlg-field"><label>Reason for cancellation *</label><textarea class="inp" id="qa-cancel-reason" rows="3" placeholder="e.g. Wrong supplier chosen, duplicate entry, vendor cancelled, etc."></textarea></div>',
        confirmText:'Cancel '+module.toUpperCase(),
        danger:true,
        onConfirm:function(){
          const reason=(($('qa-cancel-reason')||{}).value||'').trim();
          if(!reason){ reqBad('qa-cancel-reason','Cancellation reason is required'); return false; }
          r.cancelReason=reason;
          r.cancelledAt=Date.now();
          r.cancelledBy=(authUser()&&(authUser().name||authUser().username))||'unknown';
          r._qaAskingCancel=false;
          r.status=status;r.updatedAt=Date.now();
          auditPush(module,'Status changed',r.no,['Status → Cancelled (Reason: ' + reason + ')']);
          if(saveDB()){ toast(r.no+' cancelled — kept in system as audit record', r.no?'green':'green'); render(); }
        },
        onCancel:function(){ r._qaAskingCancel=false; },
        after:function(){ const ta=$('qa-cancel-reason'); if(ta) ta.focus(); r._qaAskingCancel=true; }
      });
      return;
    }
    r.status=status;r.updatedAt=Date.now();
    // If re-submitting after rejection, clear the old rejection reason so it doesn't show on the new submission
    if (status === 'Submitted' && r.rejectReason) {
      r.previousRejectReason = r.rejectReason;
      r.previousRejectedBy = r.rejectedBy;
      r.previousRejectedAt = r.rejectedAt;
      r.rejectReason = null; r.rejectedBy = null; r.rejectedAt = null;
    }
    // If re-opening from Cancelled back to Draft, preserve the old cancel
    // reason under previousCancelReason and clear the live cancel fields
    if (status === 'Draft' && r.cancelledAt) {
      r.previousCancelReason = r.cancelReason;
      r.previousCancelledBy = r.cancelledBy;
      r.previousCancelledAt = r.cancelledAt;
      r.cancelReason = null; r.cancelledBy = null; r.cancelledAt = null;
    }
    auditPush(module,'Status changed',r.no,['Status → ' + status + (status==='Rejected' && r.rejectReason ? ' (Reason: ' + r.rejectReason + ')' : '') + (status==='Cancelled' && r.cancelReason ? ' (Reason: ' + r.cancelReason + ')' : '')]);
    if(saveDB()){toast(r.no+' changed to '+status,'green');render();}
  };
  function qaWorkflowButtons(){
    const a=$('tb-actions');if(!a||!pageView||!pageView.id)return;
    const m=pageView.m,r=(db[m]||[]).find(x=>x.id===pageView.id);if(!r)return;
    const add=(label,status,cls)=>{if(a.querySelector('[data-qa-status="'+status+'"]'))return;const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='btn '+(cls||'btn-ghost');b.dataset.qaStatus=status;b.textContent=label;b.onclick=()=>qaSetStatus(m,r.id,status);a.prepend(b);};
    
    // v5: removed Inward QC workflow (Pending QC, QC Pass/Reject buttons)
    
    // v5: Cancel & Re-open — every module whose STATUSES includes 'Cancelled'.
    // Cancel requires a reason; works on Drafts as well as Approved/Completed/etc.
    // (delete is blocked for posted records, but Cancel is the supported way to
    // mark them out of the way.) Re-open takes a Cancelled record back to Draft.
    const statusList = STATUSES[m] || [];
    const supportsCancel = statusList.indexOf('Cancelled') > -1;
    // v5 QA: Cancel button removed from all module form toolbars (user request).
    if (supportsCancel && r.status === 'Cancelled') {
      add('Re-open', 'Draft', 'btn-ghost');
    }
  }

  // v5: dropdown panel for notifications (Shopify-style)
  window.qaCloseDropdowns=function(){
    qsa('.qa-dropdown-panel').forEach(function(p){p.remove();});
    document.removeEventListener('click', qaDropdownOutsideHandler, true);
  };
  function qaDropdownOutsideHandler(ev){
    if(ev.target.closest('.qa-dropdown-panel') || ev.target.closest('[data-qa-dropdown]')) return;
    window.qaCloseDropdowns();
  }
  window.qaNotifications=function(e){
    if(e)e.stopPropagation();
    window.qaCloseDropdowns();
    const due=qaPoNotifList();
    qaMarkNotifsRead();
    const bell=qs('.globalbar .gb-btn');
    if(!bell)return;
    const rect=bell.getBoundingClientRect();
    const panel=document.createElement('div');
    panel.className='qa-dropdown-panel qa-notif-panel';
    function ago(ts){
      if(!ts) return '';
      const d=Date.now()-ts;
      if(d<60000) return 'just now';
      if(d<3600000) return Math.floor(d/60000)+' min ago';
      if(d<86400000) return Math.floor(d/3600000)+' hr ago';
      return new Date(ts).toLocaleString();
    }
    const items=due.length?due.map(function(x){
      const meta=[x.no, x.by?('by '+x.by):'', ago(x.at)].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
      return '<div class="qa-notif-item" data-qa-jump="'+esc(x.m||'sale')+'" data-qa-id="'+esc(x.recId||x.id||'')+'"><div class="qa-notif-type">'+esc(x.type)+'</div><div class="qa-notif-meta">'+esc(meta)+(x.note?(' — '+esc(x.note)):'')+'</div></div>';
    }).join(''):'<div class="qa-empty" style="padding:16px;color:#6b7280">Nothing to report yet.</div>';
    panel.innerHTML='<div class="qa-dropdown-head"><strong>Notifications</strong><div style="font-size:11px;color:#6b7280;font-weight:400">Workspace alerts — all users</div></div>'+items;
    panel.style.cssText='position:fixed;top:'+(rect.bottom+8)+'px;right:'+(window.innerWidth-rect.right)+'px;width:340px;max-height:420px;overflow-y:auto;background:#fff;border-radius:10px;box-shadow:0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,.25);z-index:9000;padding:6px;color:#1f2937';
    document.body.appendChild(panel);
    setTimeout(function(){document.addEventListener('click', qaDropdownOutsideHandler, true);},0);
    qsa('.qa-notif-item', panel).forEach(function(item){
      item.style.cssText='padding:10px 12px;border-radius:6px;cursor:pointer;border-bottom:1px solid #e5e7eb';
      item.onmouseenter=function(){item.style.background='#f3f4f6'};
      item.onmouseleave=function(){item.style.background='transparent'};
      item.onclick=function(){
        const m=item.getAttribute('data-qa-jump'), id=item.getAttribute('data-qa-id');
        window.qaCloseDropdowns();
        go(m);
        setTimeout(function(){ pageView={kind:'form',m:m,id:id}; render(); },50);
      };
    });
    const head=qs('.qa-dropdown-head', panel);
    if(head){head.style.cssText='padding:10px 12px;border-bottom:1px solid #e5e7eb;font-size:14px;color:#111827';}
  };
  // v5: dropdown panel for account menu (Shopify-style)
  window.qaAccountMenu=function(e){
    if(e)e.stopPropagation();
    window.qaCloseDropdowns();
    const u=authUser();
    const av=qs('.globalbar .gb-avatar');
    if(!av)return;
    const rect=av.getBoundingClientRect();
    const panel=document.createElement('div');
    panel.className='qa-dropdown-panel qa-acct-panel';
    const initials=(u&&(u.name||u.username||'U')||'U').split(/\s+/).map(function(x){return x[0]||'';}).join('').slice(0,2).toUpperCase();
    const role=esc((u&&u.role)||'User');
    const name=esc((u&&(u.name||u.username))||'User');
    const username=esc((u&&u.username)||'');
    panel.innerHTML=
      '<div class="qa-acct-head">'+
        '<div class="qa-acct-av">'+initials+'</div>'+
        '<div class="qa-acct-info"><div class="qa-acct-name">'+name+'</div><div class="qa-acct-user">@'+username+'</div></div>'+
      '</div>'+
      '<div class="qa-acct-actions">'+
        '<button class="qa-acct-btn qa-acct-signout" id="qa-signout">Sign out</button>'+
      '</div>';
    panel.style.cssText='position:fixed;top:'+(rect.bottom+8)+'px;right:'+(window.innerWidth-rect.right)+'px;width:280px;background:#fff;border-radius:10px;box-shadow:0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,.25);z-index:9000;padding:6px;color:#1f2937';
    document.body.appendChild(panel);
    setTimeout(function(){document.addEventListener('click', qaDropdownOutsideHandler, true);},0);
    // style internals
    const head=qs('.qa-acct-head', panel);
    if(head) head.style.cssText='display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:12px;border-bottom:1px solid #e5e7eb';
    const aav=qs('.qa-acct-av', panel);
    if(aav) aav.style.cssText='width:40px;height:40px;border-radius:8px;background:#36fba1;color:#0c5132;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-weight:700;font-size:14px';
    const info=qs('.qa-acct-info', panel);
    if(info) info.style.cssText='flex:1;min-width:0';
    const an=qs('.qa-acct-name', panel);
    if(an) an.style.cssText='font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:#111827;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis';
    const au=qs('.qa-acct-user', panel);
    if(au) au.style.cssText='font-size:12px;color:#6b7280';
    const role2=qs('.qa-acct-role', panel);
    if(role2) role2.style.cssText='padding:10px 12px;font-size:12px;color:#6b7280;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.5px';
    const actions=qs('.qa-acct-actions', panel);
    if(actions) actions.style.cssText='padding:8px;border-top:1px solid #e5e7eb';
    const so=qs('.qa-acct-signout', panel);
    if(so){
      so.style.cssText='width:100%;padding:8px 12px;border:1px solid #fca5a5;background:#fef2f2;color:#b91c1c;border-radius:6px;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer';
      so.onmouseenter=function(){so.style.background='#fee2e2'};
      so.onmouseleave=function(){so.style.background='#fef2f2'};
      so.onclick=function(){window.qaCloseDropdowns(); authLogout();};
    }
  };

  function qaFixUi(){
    qaWorkflowButtons();
    const loginCard=qs('.auth-login-card');if(loginCard&&!loginCard.querySelector('.qa-forgot')){const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='btn btn-ghost qa-forgot';b.style.cssText='width:100%;margin-top:8px';b.textContent='Forgot PIN?';b.onclick=function(ev){ev.preventDefault();ev.stopPropagation();qaShowForgotPinDialog();return false;};const submit=loginCard.querySelector('.login-submit');if(submit)submit.after(b);}
    const bell=qs('.globalbar .gb-btn');if(bell){bell.setAttribute('aria-label','Notifications and action centre');bell.setAttribute('data-qa-dropdown','bell');bell.onclick=qaNotifications;bell.title='Notifications and action centre';qaRefreshBellBadge();}
    const av=qs('.globalbar .gb-avatar');if(av){av.setAttribute('aria-label','Open account menu');av.setAttribute('data-qa-dropdown','avatar');av.onclick=qaAccountMenu;av.title='Account menu';}
    qsa('[role="button"][tabindex="0"]').forEach(el=>{if(el.dataset.qaKey)return;el.dataset.qaKey='1';el.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();el.click();}});});
    qsa('button:not([aria-label])').forEach(b=>{if(!String(b.textContent||'').trim()&&b.title)b.setAttribute('aria-label',b.title);});
    qsa('input,select,textarea').forEach(el=>{if(el.getAttribute('aria-label')||el.id&&qs('label[for="'+el.id+'"]'))return;const lbl=el.closest('.field')&&qs('.lbl',el.closest('.field'));el.setAttribute('aria-label',(lbl&&lbl.textContent.trim())||el.placeholder||el.title||'Input field');if(el.maxLength<0&&el.type==='text')el.maxLength=250;});
  }
  const qaObs=new MutationObserver(function(){clearTimeout(qaUiTimer);qaUiTimer=setTimeout(qaFixUi,30);});qaObs.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});setTimeout(qaFixUi,50);

  function qaDraftKey(){return 'stitchflow_draft_'+String(cur||'')+'_'+String((pageView&&pageView.id)||'new');}
  function qaSaveDraft(){if(!qaDirty)return;const vals={};qsa('.entry-page input[id],.entry-page textarea[id]').forEach(el=>{if(el.type!=='file'&&!el.readOnly)vals[el.id]=el.value;});try{sessionStorage.setItem(qaDraftKey(),JSON.stringify({at:Date.now(),vals:vals}));}catch(_) {}}
  function qaClearDraft(){try{sessionStorage.removeItem(qaDraftKey());}catch(_) {}}
  document.addEventListener('input',function(e){if(e.target&&e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('.entry-page'))qaDirty=true;},true);
  setInterval(qaSaveDraft,5000);
  window.addEventListener('beforeunload',function(e){if(qaDirty){qaSaveDraft();e.preventDefault();e.returnValue='You have unsaved changes.';}});
  // v5: unsaved-warning guard — track whether we've already prompted for this dirty session
  let qaWarnedThisSession = false;
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    if(!qaDirty) { qaWarnedThisSession = false; return; }
    // Only warn for clicks on nav items or backlink; ignore all other clicks (typing etc.)
    const nav = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.nav-item,.backlink');
    if(!nav) return;
    // If we already warned and user already chose to discard in this session, don't re-prompt
    if(qaWarnedThisSession) {
      // user already said OK to discard - save draft and clear dirty so we don't keep blocking
      qaSaveDraft();
      qaDirty = false;
      qaWarnedThisSession = false;
      return;
    }
    qaWarnedThisSession = true;
    if(!confirm('You have unsaved changes. Discard them?')){
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    } else {
      qaSaveDraft();
      qaDirty = false;
      // reset the guard so a future dirty session can prompt again
      qaWarnedThisSession = false;
    }
  },true);

  // Configurable 30-minute idle sign-out and failed-login throttling.
  let qaLastActive=Date.now();['pointerdown','keydown','touchstart'].forEach(n=>document.addEventListener(n,()=>qaLastActive=Date.now(),true));
  setInterval(function(){if(authUser()&&Date.now()-qaLastActive>30*60*1000){toast('Signed out after 30 minutes of inactivity','red');authLogout();}},60000);
  // v5: failed-login throttling — after 5 failed attempts, lock login for 5 minutes
  // and disable username, PIN and sign-in button until lock expires
  const QA_LOGIN_FAIL_KEY = 'stitchflow_login_fail';
  // v5: self-contained dialog for Forgot PIN — works even on login screen
  function qaShowForgotPinDialog(){
    // Remove any existing one
    const existing = $('qa-forgot-dialog');
    if (existing) existing.remove();
    const ov = document.createElement('div');
    ov.id = 'qa-forgot-dialog';
    ov.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(0,0,0,.55);display:flex;align-items:flex-start;justify-content:center;padding:48px 18px;z-index:99999;overflow-y:auto';
    ov.innerHTML =
      '<div style="background:#fff;border-radius:14px;width:100%;max-width:440px;box-shadow:0 10px 40px rgba(0,0,0,.3);overflow:hidden;animation:pop .16s ease">' +
        '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;padding:16px 20px;border-bottom:1px solid #e5e7eb">' +
          '<h3 style="margin:0;font-size:18px;font-weight:600;color:#111827">Recover access</h3>' +
          '<button id="qa-forgot-close-x" type="button" style="background:transparent;border:none;cursor:pointer;padding:4px;color:#6b7280;font-size:20px;line-height:1" aria-label="Close">×</button>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<div style="padding:20px;color:#374151;font-size:14px;line-height:1.55">' +
          'Contact your StitchFlow administrator to verify your identity and reset your PIN. For security, PINs cannot be recovered or displayed.' +
        '</div>' +
        '<div style="display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;gap:8px;padding:14px 20px;border-top:1px solid #e5e7eb;background:#f9fafb">' +
          '<button id="qa-forgot-ok" type="button" style="padding:8px 16px;border:1px solid #086da8;background:#086da8;color:#fff;border-radius:6px;cursor:pointer;font-size:13px;font-weight:600">Close</button>' +
        '</div>' +
      '</div>';
    document.body.appendChild(ov);
    const closeDialog = function(){
      ov.remove();
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', escHandler, true);
    };
    const escHandler = function(e){ if (e.key === 'Escape') closeDialog(); };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', escHandler, true);
    $('qa-forgot-ok').onclick = closeDialog;
    $('qa-forgot-close-x').onclick = closeDialog;
    ov.addEventListener('click', function(e){ if (e.target === ov) closeDialog(); });
  }
  window.qaShowForgotPinDialog = qaShowForgotPinDialog;

  let qaLoginLockTimer = null;
  function qaGetLoginFailState(){
    try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(QA_LOGIN_FAIL_KEY) || '{"n":0,"until":0}'); }
    catch(e){ return {n:0,until:0}; }
  }
  function qaSetLoginFailState(s){
    try { localStorage.setItem(QA_LOGIN_FAIL_KEY, JSON.stringify(s)); } catch(e){}
  }
  function qaFormatCountdown(ms){
    const totalSec = Math.ceil(ms/1000);
    const m = Math.floor(totalSec/60);
    const s = totalSec % 60;
    return m + ' minute' + (m===1?'':'s') + ' ' + (s<10?'0':'') + s + ' second' + (s===1?'':'s');
  }
  function qaIsLoginLocked(){
    const st = qaGetLoginFailState();
    return Date.now() < st.until;
  }
  function qaApplyLoginLockUI(){
    if (qaLoginLockTimer) { clearInterval(qaLoginLockTimer); qaLoginLockTimer = null; }
    const userEl = $('login-user');
    const pinEl = $('login-pin');
    const submitBtn = qs('.auth-login-card .login-submit');
    const forgotBtn = qs('.auth-login-card .qa-forgot');
    const msg = $('login-msg');
    const update = function(){
      if (qaIsLoginLocked()){
        const st = qaGetLoginFailState();
        const remaining = st.until - Date.now();
        if (userEl) { userEl.disabled = true; userEl.style.opacity = '0.55'; userEl.style.cursor = 'not-allowed'; }
        if (pinEl)  { pinEl.disabled = true;  pinEl.style.opacity  = '0.55'; pinEl.style.cursor  = 'not-allowed'; }
        if (submitBtn) {
          submitBtn.disabled = true;
          submitBtn.style.opacity = '0.55';
          submitBtn.style.cursor = 'not-allowed';
          submitBtn.style.background = '#9ca3af';
          submitBtn.textContent = 'Locked — try in ' + qaFormatCountdown(remaining);
        }
        if (forgotBtn) { forgotBtn.disabled = true; forgotBtn.style.opacity = '0.55'; }
        if (msg) {
          msg.innerHTML = '<b>Login temporarily locked.</b> Too many failed attempts. You can try again in <b>' + qaFormatCountdown(remaining) + '</b>.';
          msg.className = 'fhint bad';
        }
      } else {
        if (userEl) { userEl.disabled = false; userEl.style.opacity = ''; userEl.style.cursor = ''; }
        if (pinEl)  { pinEl.disabled = false;  pinEl.style.opacity  = ''; pinEl.style.cursor  = ''; }
        if (submitBtn) {
          submitBtn.disabled = false;
          submitBtn.style.opacity = '';
          submitBtn.style.cursor = '';
          submitBtn.style.background = '';
          submitBtn.textContent = 'Sign in';
        }
        if (forgotBtn) { forgotBtn.disabled = false; forgotBtn.style.opacity = ''; }
      }
    };
    update();
    if (qaIsLoginLocked()){
      qaLoginLockTimer = setInterval(update, 1000);
      // also stop the timer when expired
      setTimeout(function(){
        if (qaLoginLockTimer) { clearInterval(qaLoginLockTimer); qaLoginLockTimer = null; }
        update();
      }, qaGetLoginFailState().until - Date.now() + 100);
    }
  }
  // Run on login screen show
  const qaShowLoginOrig = showLogin;
  showLogin = function(){
    qaShowLoginOrig();
    qaApplyLoginLockUI();
  };
  // Periodic check (in case the user is sitting on the screen during unlock)
  setInterval(function(){
    if (qs('.auth-login-card')) qaApplyLoginLockUI();
  }, 1000);

  const qaAuthLogin=authLogin;
  authLogin = function(){
    // Block submit while locked
    if (qaIsLoginLocked()){
      qaApplyLoginLockUI();
      return;
    }
    const before = authUser();
    qaAuthLogin();
    const after = authUser();
    if (!after && !before){
      // failed attempt
      const state = qaGetLoginFailState();
      state.n = (state.n || 0) + 1;
      if (state.n >= 5){
        state.until = Date.now() + 5*60*1000;  // 5 minutes
        state.n = 0;
      }
      qaSetLoginFailState(state);
      // refresh lock UI
      qaApplyLoginLockUI();
      // show warning toast with remaining attempts
      if (state.n === 0){
        toast('Too many failed attempts. Login is locked for 5 minutes.', 'red');
      } else {
        const remaining = 5 - state.n;
        toast('Invalid username/PIN. ' + remaining + ' attempt' + (remaining===1?'':'s') + ' remaining before lockout.', 'red');
      }
    } else if (after){
      // success - clear the fail counter
      qaSetLoginFailState({n:0, until:0});
      qaApplyLoginLockUI();
    }
  };

  // Repair sequences from existing IDs, including reset-created users.
  db.seq=db.seq||{};db.seq.user=Math.max(pf(db.seq.user),(db.users||[]).reduce((m,u)=>Math.max(m,parseInt(String(u.id||'').replace(/\D/g,''))||0),0));
  let qaIdsRepaired=false;
  (db.users||[]).forEach(function(u,i,arr){if(arr.findIndex(x=>x.id===u.id)!==i){db.seq.user++;u.id='USR-'+String(db.seq.user).padStart(4,'0');qaIdsRepaired=true;}});
  if(qaIdsRepaired)saveDB();

})();
</script>
